They start neither CEF nor an AVKit window; the parity tests make WebKit web views without a page. `swift test --filter ARCHEngineTests` runs the engine helper tests (Swift Testing): when a tab keeps its icon as its address changes, that icons, and other images a page names with a limit of their own, load without cookies or stored credentials, and which permissions need a capture device, without reading or requesting the system's permission; how a key that the engines' trusted input presses reads as macOS describes it: the US layout's key codes, the characters with Shift and Control, which presses type a character, and the keys DOM names, the wheel deltas both adapters send, rounded, clamped to ±100,000 points, and refused when not finite; a file chooser's rules: the accepted types a request keeps, their file types for a panel or none when one has no declared type, and what the page receives of an answer; and how a page context menu is shown so that AppKit adds its own items: which view AppKit is asked about for each element, a field's text input view only while it has a text input context, a password field's while the engine's AutoFill call, which only a field's menu turns on, is on, the page's text input view found under a click from the frontmost view, the click's own event or the right mouse down the menu opens at, and the address a clicked link hands Services when AppKit asks by the current or the older type names, on a pasteboard of the test's own, without a window or a menu on the screen. `swift test --filter ARCHEngineWebKitTests` runs the WebKit adapter tests (Swift Testing): the web view of a tab and of a popup knows the web contents that made it, and a page script's message reaches that web contents' page script delegate; closing all of a browser's web contents ends each page; a cookie keeps its SameSite policy and its other contents through a profile's cookie store; a page's readable content holds the text selected outside editable areas and no selection that reaches one, also in an open shadow tree; a profile's web extensions hear that a window whose tab moves out closes after the tab has moved, with the active tab and focus following, that a tab listed in the place of the tab whose web contents made it replaces that tab, and of only the first window of an identity that a report names twice; an extension's `tabs.update` to its own page on a web page's tab, a link and a typed address from its options page to a web page each load in a replacement that the web contents hands its UI delegate, while the extension's own pages still load in place, and a page of an extension that loads later waits for it and then loads in a replacement, unless another navigation ends the wait first; a web page's link and script reach no extension page that the extension does not make web-accessible to the site, while a link to one it does and the app's own load each load in a replacement, a frame of the extension may open any of its pages, and `web_accessible_resources` is read as Chromium reads it; a profile without a storage identifier uses WebKit's default store, while the tests' profile, over `WKWebsiteDataStore.nonPersistent()`, and its web extensions keep nothing on disk; a download's pause, resume, and cancel meet WebKit's late reports in `WebKitDownloadLifecycle` (a pause that meets the download's finish, a resume while the pause still stops the download, a second resume before WebKit restarts it, a cancel before the restarted download arrives, and a failed download with and without resume data); find reports the selected match, or steps from the same search's last match and wraps around, and counts no match across blocks; a dialog takes one answer, the user's or the safe answer when a navigation comes first, and the hold on the page's further dialogs that the navigation sets ends when loading stops; the context menu keeps WebKit's editing and spelling items by tag, with WebKit's menus built by hand; against a server on the loopback address, the navigation delegate hears of each main frame navigation and each redirect hop before its request, and a cancelled hop's request never leaves, while a frame inside the page asks about nothing; and on a page in a window that is never ordered in, so none is key or on screen, the trusted input's clicks with no pointer move first, also at twice the page's zoom, keys while the web view is its window's first responder, and text, also outside any window, reach the page with `isTrusted` true, a key down that the page leaves unhandled comes back through `NSApplication.sendEvent(_:)` as the sent event, which the adapter knows as its input's, also past the eighth key, and one that comes back through the window goes neither to the page again nor up the responder chain (the app's filter is left to check 84), input that could reach the menus or pass through the user's input source, or that needs the key window or a window, is refused, and a wheel delta is clamped or, when not finite, refused; and on such a page a file input's chooser reaches the UI delegate with its mode and accepted types, also from a script's `click()` in an app's world, and with the asking frame's origin and the page's committed one, also while the page loads another address and while a restored tab with a frame goes back, and the page receives the chosen file, several files, or a folder's files, only the first for an input that takes one, and a cancel for nil, for addresses that are not files, for a delegate that drops the completion, and without a delegate; every wait fails after a few seconds, also when the page never answers. Only these tests make such a profile, through the adapter's internal `makeBrowserContext(storageIdentifier:dataStore:)`. They load no page from the network and start WebKit web content processes. `swift test --filter ARCHPrivateAPITests` runs the private API flag tests (Swift Testing): every flag is on until the user turns it off, a registered API is checked on a background queue at once, a feature runs only while the flag for all private APIs, its own flag, and each of its APIs' flags are on and each API exists, and a check that a view's drawing asks for is recorded after that turn of the main actor; they keep the flags in a defaults domain for each test, named `test.arch.private-api-flags.<checkout>.<test>` after a hash of the checkout's path and the test and reused on every run in the checkout, so that runs in two checkouts at the same time keep apart. `swift test --filter AgentControlTests` runs the agents' control channel tests (Swift Testing): framing, JSON-RPC, `hello`, the tool catalog, the untrusted wrapper, the socket location, the bundle layout, the command line with `spaces`, `tabs`, `release`, the acting commands, `task`, and `sessions`, the exit codes and the client errors they report, the harness, the client against a listener in a temporary folder that stands in for Arch, whose code check passes for the test process's own code, the MCP core against transcripts with the recorded starts of Claude Code and Codex, the MCP configurations, and the link installer in a temporary home folder, with an Arch bundle's own `Contents/Helpers` and a folder that PATH spells twice among its folders; they reach no Arch and no network. `MCPExecutableTests` runs the `arch-cli` that `swift build` wrote beside the test bundles through pipes, as `arch-mcp` through a link and as `arch-cli mcp`, with `ARCH_AGENT_TEST_SOCKET` naming a path in the temporary folder where nothing listens, so a running Arch never sees them; `swift test` does not build that executable, so `swift build` runs first. `swift test --filter AgentTests` runs the agents' service tests (Swift Testing) against a service whose files and socket are in a temporary folder, with a browser host, a grant prompt, an authenticator, and a seal key in memory, a clock of the test's, and this test process as both ends, whose own code the peer check expects: the endpoint (refused peers and their log entries, another user, the folder rules, a stale socket, listening only while needed, a client's check of the listener, `ready` first, the recheck before each frame, `hello`'s refusals, and methods before `hello`), grants (the sheet at the first call, the Agents Persona, Touch ID, the deny rate limit, revocation, release, expiry, a process that ended, a deleted Persona, and outside access turned off), the seal (read back, an edited, unreadable, or keyless file set aside, a key the Keychain does not give, and a later build's members), client identity over a process table in memory and the system's reader against the test process, the activity log, the tool host over a fake browser host whose engine asks before each address and redirect hop and answers on the next turn (`AgentToolHostTests`: agent tabs, events, limits and their messages, statuses, excluded sites at navigation, at redirect hops, and at read time, internal pages, P17, a dialog's text only from a frame on the page's site that AI may read, and `search_history`'s scope), the policy table for every rule in every mode for in-app sessions and outside clients with the allowances, Enter or Space by `press` on a control as a click on it, P5's sites as registrable domains, the scope rule, and the word lists (`PolicyTests`), the cards (`ApprovalTests`: grouping per page, withdrawal, also on a document that commits and never finishes, timeouts, Touch ID for outside clients' sensitive Allow and Share, the warning line, ended cards leaving memory, `approvalPending` and `approval_wait`, the tools that wait for the user, the cards a page's download or link to another app opens, one download card per tab, and, through the service and the socket, Stop All Agents, revocation, the client leaving, quit, a client's `tools/cancel` of a call and of an `approval_wait`, which withdraws the card of the call it waits on, a grant allowed after its only call was cancelled showing its client as connected, the log and step of a call that goes on after `approvalPending`, and the click check on sources read by their running code and on a real button that an accessibility press does not press), and the page script in WebKit web views without a window over a non-persistent store on the pages in `Fixtures`, loaded at an https address with no request leaving (`PageScriptTests`: the outline, refs and fingerprints, every redaction rule, shared tabs, hidden elements, shadow roots, frames, stale refs, the diff and the cut, the boxes and a screenshot with a filled password field painted over, fields and frames inside `aria-hidden` and `inert` subtrees painted over, every field of a shared tab painted over with no scroll before the user decides, a page that replaced built-ins, actionability and covers, typing into a framework-style controlled input, selects, Enter submission with the default button under P4 and P5, no user activation in the page's handlers, a search form's hidden input, the element facts the rules read, a page whose address changes before its document finishes, captures that never answer, no full-page screenshot of a Chromium tab with boxes, a call stopped before its card, and the modes), in-app sessions over a fake runner (tickets from the session's process tree only, while it lives, in client and over the tool host with `guide`'s answers, and the agent run's values' JSON against fixed text; they reach no Keychain, visible window, or Arch, and the page script tests start WebKit web content processes. `swift test --filter AITests` runs the AI tests (Swift Testing), which use a `URLProtocol` stub, an in-memory key store, and shell scripts written to temporary folders as command-line tools, so they reach neither the network, the Keychain, nor a real AI tool, and they check that the providers' session follows redirects only within the base URL's origin and that the `AgentSessionTests` runs shell scripts that answer as `claude` and `codex` and replay those streams: the exact arguments and environments of sessions, follow-ups, efforts, skill folders, and ordinary requests, the start check refusing each failing `init` and passing a later one after a mode change, the watcher, the feature derivation, the configuration probe naming its group, probes run again for found only through the step's route. `swift test --filter EventsTests` runs the browser event tests (Swift Testing), which need no session: each payload's, an event's, a cause's, and a filter's JSON
